# ERRORS.md — the ERROR-SURFACE TABLE

Every distinct way the C in `c_src/src/` rejects or errors on input, derived
mechanically from the source: every `RETURN_ERROR`/`RETURN_ERROR_IF`/`ERROR(...)`
site, every `return NULL`, every `BOUNDCHECK`, every explicit range/null/stage
check, and every min/max constant that gates acceptance. One row per rejection
site — three `RETURN_ERROR` branches in one function are three rows.

`assert()` is compiled out in this build (`DEBUGLEVEL` is 0), so an assertion is
only a row when the same condition also has a real error return. Where an
assertion is the *only* guard, the C has undefined behaviour on that input; those
cases are called out in the `reach` column and in the exclusions section, because
there is no C behaviour for the Rust to match.

## Build configuration these rows were derived under

From `c_src/CMakeLists.txt`: `ZSTD_LEGACY_SUPPORT=5`, `XXH_NAMESPACE=ZSTD_`,
`DYNAMIC_BMI2=0`, no `ZSTD_MULTITHREAD`, no `-mavx2`/`-mbmi2`, `-w`, and no
`CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE` (so `-O0`). Consequences that change the error surface:

* `DEBUGLEVEL=0` -> every `assert()` is `((void)0)`.
* No `ZSTD_MULTITHREAD` -> `ZSTD_c_nbWorkers`/`jobSize`/`overlapLog`/`rsyncable`
  have bounds `{0,0}` and reject any non-zero value with `parameter_unsupported`
  (40); `ZSTDMT_createCCtx_advanced` returns `NULL` unconditionally, so
  `cctx->mtctx` is permanently `NULL` and the whole ZSTDMT error surface is
  unreachable; `POOL_*` are the synchronous stubs.
* `ZSTD_LEGACY_SUPPORT=5` -> `ZSTD_isLegacy` only recognises v05/v06/v07, so a
  v0.1-v0.4 magic number yields `prefix_unknown` (10), *not*
  `version_unsupported` (12). `zstd_v01.c`..`zstd_v04.c` are still compiled and
  still export their symbols, so they remain directly callable.
* `DYNAMIC_BMI2=0` and no `__BMI2__` -> `ZSTD_ENABLE_ASM_X86_64_BMI2 == 0`: no
  Huffman assembly, and every `_bmi2` dispatcher takes the `_default` path.
  `HUF_DISABLE_FAST_DECODE` is *not* set, so the Huffman fast **C** decode loops
  are live and their rejection sites are reachable.
* `ZSTD_COMPRESS_HEAPMODE` defaults to 0, so `ZSTD_compress`'s heap-allocation
  failure path is compiled out.

## Column meanings

| column | meaning |
|---|---|
| `file:line` | the exact rejection site in `c_src/src/` |
| `function` | the C function containing it |
| `trigger` | the precise invalid input/condition, with macro constants resolved to numbers |
| `expected C result` | the error code / sentinel the C returns (numeric `ZSTD_error_*` value where one exists) |
| `public entry point + concrete input` | an exported symbol plus an input that reaches the site |
| `reach` | `DIRECT` = an exported call hits it; `INDIRECT` = only through a higher-level API; `UNREACHABLE` = defensive/internal invariant no public input can trigger; `UNSAFE-UB` = the reference C has undefined behaviour on this input, so it is excluded from the differential tests |

| compress/zstd_compress.c, lines 1-4000 | 130 |
| compress/zstd_compress.c, lines 4000-7843 | 117 |
| compress/ (everything except zstd_compress.c) | 195 |
| decompress/ | 309 |
| dictBuilder/, deprecated/ and legacy/ | 338 |
| **total** | **1142** |
